^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   17) /// A specialized [multimap](<https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Multimap>) for
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   18) /// header names and values.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   19) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   20) /// # Overview
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   21) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   22) /// `HeaderMap` is designed specifically for efficient manipulation of HTTP
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   23) /// headers. It supports multiple values per header name and provides
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   24) /// specialized APIs for insertion, retrieval, and iteration.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   25) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   26) /// The internal implementation is optimized for common usage patterns in HTTP,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   27) /// and may change across versions. For example, the current implementation uses
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   28) /// [Robin Hood
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   29) /// hashing](<https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hash_table#Robin_Hood_hashing>) to
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   30) /// store entries compactly and enable high load factors with good performance.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   31) /// However, the collision resolution strategy and storage mechanism are not
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   32) /// part of the public API and may be altered in future releases.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   33) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   34) /// # Iteration order
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   35) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   36) /// Unless otherwise specified, the order in which items are returned by
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   37) /// iterators from `HeaderMap` methods is arbitrary; there is no guaranteed
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   38) /// ordering among the elements yielded by such an iterator. Changes to the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   39) /// iteration order are not considered breaking changes, so users must not rely
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   40) /// on any incidental order produced by such an iterator. However, for a given
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   41) /// crate version, the iteration order will be consistent across all platforms.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   42) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   43) /// # Adaptive hashing
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   44) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   45) /// `HeaderMap` uses an adaptive strategy for hashing to maintain fast lookups
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   46) /// while resisting hash collision attacks. The default hash function
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   47) /// prioritizes performance. In scenarios where high collision rates are
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   48) /// detected—typically indicative of denial-of-service attacks—the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   49) /// implementation switches to a more secure, collision-resistant hash function.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   50) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   51) /// # Limitations
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   52) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   53) /// A `HeaderMap` can hold a limited number of entries, currently 24,576 header
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   54) /// name/value pairs. Methods that would grow the map beyond that limit, such as
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   55) /// [`insert`](Self::insert), [`append`](Self::append), and
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   56) /// [`reserve`](Self::reserve), panic once it is reached. The fallible
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   57) /// counterparts [`try_insert`](Self::try_insert),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   58) /// [`try_append`](Self::try_append), and [`try_reserve`](Self::try_reserve)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   59) /// return a [`MaxSizeReached`] error instead, so callers can handle the limit
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   60) /// without panicking.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   61) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   62) /// [`HeaderName`]: struct.HeaderName.html
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   63) /// [`HeaderMap`]: struct.HeaderMap.html
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   64) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   65) /// # Examples
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   66) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   67) /// Basic usage
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   68) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   69) /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   70) /// # use http::HeaderMap;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   71) /// # use http::header::{CONTENT_LENGTH, HOST, LOCATION};
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   72) /// let mut headers = HeaderMap::new();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   73) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   74) /// headers.insert(HOST, "example.com".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   75) /// headers.insert(CONTENT_LENGTH, "123".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   76) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   77) /// assert!(headers.contains_key(HOST));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   78) /// assert!(!headers.contains_key(LOCATION));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   79) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   80) /// assert_eq!(headers[HOST], "example.com");
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   81) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   82) /// headers.remove(HOST);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   83) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   84) /// assert!(!headers.contains_key(HOST));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   85) /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   86) #[derive(Clone)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   87) pub struct HeaderMap<T = HeaderValue> {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   90)     indices: Box<[Pos]>,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   91)     entries: Vec<Bucket<T>>,

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   96) // # Implementation notes
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   97) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   98) // Below, you will find a fairly large amount of code. Most of this is to
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200   99) // provide the necessary functions to efficiently manipulate the header
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  100) // multimap. The core hashing table is based on robin hood hashing [1]. While
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  101) // this is the same hashing algorithm used as part of Rust's `HashMap` in
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  102) // stdlib, many implementation details are different. The two primary reasons
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  103) // for this divergence are that `HeaderMap` is a multimap and the structure has
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  104) // been optimized to take advantage of the characteristics of HTTP headers.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  105) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  106) // ## Structure Layout
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  107) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  108) // Most of the data contained by `HeaderMap` is *not* stored in the hash table.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  109) // Instead, pairs of header name and *first* associated header value are stored
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  110) // in the `entries` vector. If the header name has more than one associated
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  111) // header value, then additional values are stored in `extra_values`. The actual
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  112) // hash table (`indices`) only maps hash codes to indices in `entries`. This
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  113) // means that, when an eviction happens, the actual header name and value stay
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  114) // put and only a tiny amount of memory has to be copied.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  115) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  116) // Extra values associated with a header name are tracked using a linked list.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  117) // Links are formed with offsets into `extra_values` and not pointers.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  118) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  119) // [1]: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hash_table#Robin_Hood_hashing
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  120)

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  280) /// Type used for representing the size of a HeaderMap value.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  281) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  282) /// 32,768 is more than enough entries for a single header map. Setting this
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  283) /// limit enables using `u16` to represent all offsets, which takes 2 bytes
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  284) /// instead of 8 on 64 bit processors.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  285) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  286) /// Setting this limit is especially beneficial for `indices`, making it more
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  287) /// cache friendly. More hash codes can fit in a cache line.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  288) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  289) /// You may notice that `u16` may represent more than 32,768 values. This is
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  290) /// true, but 32,768 should be plenty and it allows us to reserve the top bit
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  291) /// for future usage.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  292) type Size = u16;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  293) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  294) /// This limit falls out from above.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  295) const MAX_SIZE: usize = 1 << 15;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  296) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  297) /// An entry in the hash table. This represents the full hash code for an entry
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  298) /// as well as the position of the entry in the `entries` vector.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  299) #[derive(Copy, Clone)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  300) struct Pos {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  301)     // Index in the `entries` vec
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  302)     index: Size,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  303)     // Full hash value for the entry.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  304)     hash: HashValue,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  305) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  306) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  307) /// Hash values are limited to u16 as well. While `fast_hash` and `Hasher`
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  308) /// return `usize` hash codes, limiting the effective hash code to the lower 16
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  309) /// bits is fine since we know that the `indices` vector will never grow beyond
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  310) /// that size.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  311) #[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone, Eq, PartialEq)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  312) struct HashValue(u16);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  313) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  314) /// Stores the data associated with a `HeaderMap` entry. Only the first value is
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  315) /// included in this struct. If a header name has more than one associated
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  316) /// value, all extra values are stored in the `extra_values` vector. A doubly
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  317) /// linked list of entries is maintained. The doubly linked list is used so that
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  318) /// removing a value is constant time. This also has the nice property of
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  319) /// enabling double ended iteration.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  320) #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  321) struct Bucket<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  322)     hash: HashValue,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  323)     key: HeaderName,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  324)     value: T,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  325)     links: Option<Links>,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  326) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  327) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  328) /// The head and tail of the value linked list.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  329) #[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  330) struct Links {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  331)     next: usize,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  332)     tail: usize,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  333) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  334) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  335) /// Access to the `links` value in a slice of buckets.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  336) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  337) /// It's important that no other field is accessed, since it may have been
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  338) /// freed in a `Drain` iterator.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  339) #[derive(Debug)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  340) struct RawLinks<T>(*mut [Bucket<T>]);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  341) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  342) /// Node in doubly-linked list of header value entries
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  343) #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  344) struct ExtraValue<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  345)     value: T,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  346)     prev: Link,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  347)     next: Link,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  348) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  349) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  350) /// A header value node is either linked to another node in the `extra_values`
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  351) /// list or it points to an entry in `entries`. The entry in `entries` is the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  352) /// start of the list and holds the associated header name.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  353) #[derive(Debug, Copy, Clone, Eq, PartialEq)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  354) enum Link {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  355)     Entry(usize),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  356)     Extra(usize),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  357) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  358) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  359) /// Tracks the header map danger level! This relates to the adaptive hashing
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  360) /// algorithm. A HeaderMap starts in the "green" state, when a large number of
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  361) /// collisions are detected, it transitions to the yellow state. At this point,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  362) /// the header map will either grow and switch back to the green state OR it
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  363) /// will transition to the red state.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  364) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  365) /// When in the red state, a safe hashing algorithm is used and all values in
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  366) /// the header map have to be rehashed.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  367) #[derive(Clone)]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  368) enum Danger {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  369)     Green,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  370)     Yellow,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  371)     Red(RandomState),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  372) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  373) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  374) // Constants related to detecting DOS attacks.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  375) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  376) // Displacement is the number of entries that get shifted when inserting a new
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  377) // value. Forward shift is how far the entry gets stored from the ideal
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  378) // position.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  379) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  380) // The current constant values were picked from another implementation. It could
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  381) // be that there are different values better suited to the header map case.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  382) const DISPLACEMENT_THRESHOLD: usize = 128;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  383) const FORWARD_SHIFT_THRESHOLD: usize = 512;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  384) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  385) // The default strategy for handling the yellow danger state is to increase the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  386) // header map capacity in order to (hopefully) reduce the number of collisions.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  387) // If growing the hash map would cause the load factor to drop bellow this
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  388) // threshold, then instead of growing, the headermap is switched to the red
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  389) // danger state and safe hashing is used instead.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  390) const LOAD_FACTOR_THRESHOLD: usize = 5;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  391) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  392) // Macro used to iterate the hash table starting at a given point, looping when
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  393) // the end is hit.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  394) macro_rules! probe_loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  395)     ($label:tt: $probe_var: ident < $len: expr, $body: expr) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  396)         debug_assert!($len > 0);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  397)         $label:
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  398)         loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  399)             if $probe_var < $len {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  400)                 $body
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  401)                 $probe_var += 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  402)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  403)                 $probe_var = 0;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  404)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  405)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  406)     };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  407)     ($probe_var: ident < $len: expr, $body: expr) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  408)         debug_assert!($len > 0);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  409)         loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  410)             if $probe_var < $len {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  411)                 $body
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  412)                 $probe_var += 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  413)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  414)                 $probe_var = 0;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  415)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  416)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  417)     };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  418) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  419) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  420) // First part of the robinhood algorithm. Given a key, find the slot in which it
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  421) // will be inserted. This is done by starting at the "ideal" spot. Then scanning
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  422) // until the destination slot is found. A destination slot is either the next
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  423) // empty slot or the next slot that is occupied by an entry that has a lower
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  424) // displacement (displacement is the distance from the ideal spot).
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  425) //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  426) // This is implemented as a macro instead of a function that takes a closure in
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  427) // order to guarantee that it is "inlined". There is no way to annotate closures
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  428) // to guarantee inlining.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  429) macro_rules! insert_phase_one {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  430)     ($map:ident,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  431)      $key:expr,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  432)      $probe:ident,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  433)      $pos:ident,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  434)      $hash:ident,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  435)      $danger:ident,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  436)      $vacant:expr,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  437)      $occupied:expr,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  438)      $robinhood:expr) =>
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  439)     {{
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  440)         let $hash = hash_elem_using(&$map.danger, &$key);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  441)         let mut $probe = desired_pos($map.mask, $hash);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  442)         let mut dist = 0;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  443)         let ret;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  444) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  445)         // Start at the ideal position, checking all slots
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  446)         probe_loop!('probe: $probe < $map.indices.len(), {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  447)             if let Some(($pos, entry_hash)) = $map.indices[$probe].resolve() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  448)                 // The slot is already occupied, but check if it has a lower
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  449)                 // displacement.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  450)                 let their_dist = probe_distance($map.mask, entry_hash, $probe);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  451) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  452)                 if their_dist < dist {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  453)                     // The new key's distance is larger, so claim this spot and
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  454)                     // displace the current entry.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  455)                     //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  456)                     // Check if this insertion is above the danger threshold.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  457)                     let $danger =
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  458)                         dist >= FORWARD_SHIFT_THRESHOLD && !$map.danger.is_red();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  459) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  460)                     ret = $robinhood;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  461)                     break 'probe;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  462)                 } else if entry_hash == $hash && $map.entries[$pos].key == $key {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  463)                     // There already is an entry with the same key.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  464)                     ret = $occupied;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  465)                     break 'probe;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  466)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  467)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  468)                 // The entry is vacant, use it for this key.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  469)                 let $danger =
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  470)                     dist >= FORWARD_SHIFT_THRESHOLD && !$map.danger.is_red();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  471) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  472)                 ret = $vacant;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  473)                 break 'probe;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  474)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  475) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  476)             dist += 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  477)         });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  478) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  479)         ret
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  480)     }}
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  481) }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  482)

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  518) impl<T> HeaderMap<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  519)     /// Create an empty `HeaderMap` with the specified capacity.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  520)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  521)     /// The returned map will allocate internal storage in order to hold about

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  525)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  526)     /// More capacity than requested may be allocated.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  527)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  528)     /// # Panics
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  529)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  530)     /// This method panics if capacity exceeds max `HeaderMap` capacity.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  531)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  532)     /// # Examples
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  533)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  534)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  535)     /// # use http::HeaderMap;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  536)     /// let map: HeaderMap<u32> = HeaderMap::with_capacity(10);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  537)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  538)     /// assert!(map.is_empty());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  539)     /// assert_eq!(12, map.capacity());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  540)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  541)     pub fn with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> HeaderMap<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  542)         Self::try_with_capacity(capacity).expect("size overflows MAX_SIZE")
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  543)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  544) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  545)     /// Create an empty `HeaderMap` with the specified capacity.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  546)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  547)     /// The returned map will allocate internal storage in order to hold about

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  549)     /// effort" as there are usage patterns that could cause additional

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  551)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  557)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  563)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  567)     pub fn try_with_capacity(capacity: usize) -> Result<HeaderMap<T>, MaxSizeReached> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  568)         if capacity == 0 {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  569)             Ok(Self::default())
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  570)         } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  571)             let raw_cap = to_raw_capacity(capacity)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  572)             let raw_cap = match raw_cap.checked_next_power_of_two() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  573)                 Some(c) => c,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  574)                 None => return Err(MaxSizeReached { _priv: () }),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  575)             };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  576)             if raw_cap > MAX_SIZE {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  577)                 return Err(MaxSizeReached { _priv: () });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  578)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  579)             debug_assert!(raw_cap > 0);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  580) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  581)             Ok(HeaderMap {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  582)                 mask: (raw_cap - 1) as Size,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  583)                 indices: vec![Pos::none(); raw_cap].into_boxed_slice(),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  584)                 entries: Vec::with_capacity(usable_capacity(raw_cap)),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  585)                 extra_values: Vec::new(),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  586)                 danger: Danger::Green,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  587)             })
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  588)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  589)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  590)

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  690)     /// Returns the number of headers the map can hold without reallocating.

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  694)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  707)     pub fn capacity(&self) -> usize {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  710) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  711)     /// Reserves capacity for at least `additional` more headers to be inserted
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  712)     /// into the `HeaderMap`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  713)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  714)     /// The header map may reserve more space to avoid frequent reallocations.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  715)     /// Like with `with_capacity`, this will be a "best effort" to avoid
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  716)     /// allocations until `additional` more headers are inserted. Certain usage
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  717)     /// patterns could cause additional allocations before the number is
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  718)     /// reached.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  719)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  720)     /// # Panics
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  721)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  722)     /// Panics if reserving the additional capacity would grow the map beyond
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  723)     /// its maximum capacity. See the [`HeaderMap`] documentation for the limit,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  724)     /// or use [`try_reserve`](Self::try_reserve) to handle the failure without
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  725)     /// panicking.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  726)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  727)     /// # Examples
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  728)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  729)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  730)     /// # use http::HeaderMap;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  731)     /// # use http::header::HOST;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  732)     /// let mut map = HeaderMap::new();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  733)     /// map.reserve(10);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  734)     /// # map.insert(HOST, "bar".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  735)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  736)     pub fn reserve(&mut self, additional: usize) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  737)         self.try_reserve(additional)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  738)             .expect("size overflows MAX_SIZE")
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  739)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  740) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  741)     /// Reserves capacity for at least `additional` more headers to be inserted
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  742)     /// into the `HeaderMap`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  743)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  744)     /// The header map may reserve more space to avoid frequent reallocations.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  745)     /// Like with `with_capacity`, this will be a "best effort" to avoid
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  746)     /// allocations until `additional` more headers are inserted. Certain usage

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  749)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  750)     /// # Errors
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  751)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  752)     /// This method differs from `reserve` by returning an error instead of
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  753)     /// panicking if the value is too large.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  754)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  759)     /// # use http::header::HOST;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  760)     /// let mut map = HeaderMap::new();

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  763)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  764)     pub fn try_reserve(&mut self, additional: usize) -> Result<(), MaxSizeReached> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  765)         // TODO: This can't overflow if done properly... since the max # of
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  766)         // elements is u16::MAX.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  767)         let cap = self
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  768)             .entries
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  769)             .len()
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  770)             .checked_add(additional)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  771)             .ok_or_else(MaxSizeReached::new)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  772) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  773)         let raw_cap = to_raw_capacity(cap)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  774) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  775)         if raw_cap > self.indices.len() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  776)             let raw_cap = raw_cap
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  777)                 .checked_next_power_of_two()
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  778)                 .ok_or_else(MaxSizeReached::new)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  779)             if raw_cap > MAX_SIZE {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  780)                 return Err(MaxSizeReached::new());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  781)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  782) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  783)             if self.entries.is_empty() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  784)                 self.mask = raw_cap as Size - 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  785)                 self.indices = vec![Pos::none(); raw_cap].into_boxed_slice();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  786)                 self.entries = Vec::with_capacity(usable_capacity(raw_cap));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  787)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  788)                 self.try_grow(raw_cap)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  789)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  790)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  791) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  792)         Ok(())
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  793)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200  794)

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1088)     pub fn drain(&mut self) -> Drain<'_, T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1089)         for i in self.indices.iter_mut() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1090)             *i = Pos::none();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1091)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1092) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1093)         // Memory safety
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1094)         //
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1095)         // When the Drain is first created, it shortens the length of
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1096)         // the source vector to make sure no uninitialized or moved-from
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1097)         // elements are accessible at all if the Drain's destructor never
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1098)         // gets to run.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1099) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1100)         let entries = &mut self.entries[..] as *mut _;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1101)         let extra_values = &mut self.extra_values as *mut _;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1102)         let len = self.entries.len();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1103)         unsafe {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1104)             self.entries.set_len(0);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1105)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1106) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1107)         Drain {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1108)             idx: 0,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1109)             len,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1110)             entries,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1111)             extra_values,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1112)             next: None,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1113)             lt: PhantomData,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1114)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1115)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1116) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1117)     fn value_iter(&self, idx: Option<usize>) -> ValueIter<'_, T> {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1119)

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1123)

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1132)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1133)         } else {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1143)     fn value_iter_mut(&mut self, idx: usize) -> ValueIterMut<'_, T> {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1609)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1610)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1611) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1612)     /// Remove an entry from the map.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1613)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1614)     /// Warning: To avoid inconsistent state, extra values _must_ be removed
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1615)     /// for the `found` index (via `remove_all_extra_values` or similar)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1616)     /// _before_ this method is called.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1617)     #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1618)     fn remove_found(&mut self, probe: usize, found: usize) -> Bucket<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1619)         // index `probe` and entry `found` is to be removed
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1620)         // use swap_remove, but then we need to update the index that points
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1621)         // to the other entry that has to move
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1622)         self.indices[probe] = Pos::none();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1623)         let entry = self.entries.swap_remove(found);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1624) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1625)         // correct index that points to the entry that had to swap places
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1626)         if let Some(entry) = self.entries.get(found) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1627)             // was not last element
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1628)             // examine new element in `found` and find it in indices
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1629)             let mut probe = desired_pos(self.mask, entry.hash);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1630) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1631)             probe_loop!(probe < self.indices.len(), {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1632)                 if let Some((i, _)) = self.indices[probe].resolve() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1633)                     if i >= self.entries.len() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1634)                         // found it
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1635)                         self.indices[probe] = Pos::new(found, entry.hash);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1636)                         break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1637)                     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1638)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1639)             });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1640) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1641)             // Update links
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1642)             if let Some(links) = entry.links {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1643)                 self.extra_values[links.next].prev = Link::Entry(found);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1644)                 self.extra_values[links.tail].next = Link::Entry(found);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1645)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1646)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1647) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1648)         // backward shift deletion in self.indices
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1649)         // after probe, shift all non-ideally placed indices backward
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1650)         if !self.entries.is_empty() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1651)             let mut last_probe = probe;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1652)             let mut probe = probe + 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1653) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1654)             probe_loop!(probe < self.indices.len(), {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1655)                 if let Some((_, entry_hash)) = self.indices[probe].resolve() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1656)                     if probe_distance(self.mask, entry_hash, probe) > 0 {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1657)                         self.indices[last_probe] = self.indices[probe];
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1658)                         self.indices[probe] = Pos::none();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1659)                     } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1660)                         break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1661)                     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1662)                 } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1663)                     break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1664)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1665) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1666)                 last_probe = probe;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1667)             });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1668)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1669) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1670)         entry
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1671)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1672) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1673)     /// Removes the `ExtraValue` at the given index.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1674)     #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1675)     fn remove_extra_value(&mut self, idx: usize) -> ExtraValue<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1676)         let raw_links = self.raw_links();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1677)         remove_extra_value(raw_links, &mut self.extra_values, idx)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1678)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1679) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1680)     fn remove_all_extra_values(&mut self, mut head: usize) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1681)         loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1682)             let extra = self.remove_extra_value(head);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1683) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1684)             if let Link::Extra(idx) = extra.next {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1685)                 head = idx;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1686)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1687)                 break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1688)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1689)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1690)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1691) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1692)     #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1693)     fn try_insert_entry(
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1694)         &mut self,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1695)         hash: HashValue,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1696)         key: HeaderName,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1697)         value: T,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1698)     ) -> Result<(), MaxSizeReached> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1699)         if self.entries.len() >= MAX_SIZE {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1700)             return Err(MaxSizeReached::new());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1701)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1702) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1703)         self.entries.push(Bucket {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1704)             hash,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1705)             key,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1706)             value,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1707)             links: None,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1708)         });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1709) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1710)         Ok(())
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1711)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1712) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1713)     fn rebuild(&mut self) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1714)         // Loop over all entries and re-insert them into the map
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1715)         'outer: for (index, entry) in self.entries.iter_mut().enumerate() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1716)             let hash = hash_elem_using(&self.danger, &entry.key);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1717)             let mut probe = desired_pos(self.mask, hash);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1718)             let mut dist = 0;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1719) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1720)             // Update the entry's hash code
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1721)             entry.hash = hash;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1722) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1723)             probe_loop!(probe < self.indices.len(), {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1724)                 if let Some((_, entry_hash)) = self.indices[probe].resolve() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1725)                     // if existing element probed less than us, swap
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1726)                     let their_dist = probe_distance(self.mask, entry_hash, probe);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1727) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1728)                     if their_dist < dist {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1729)                         // Robinhood
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1730)                         break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1731)                     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1732)                 } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1733)                     // Vacant slot
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1734)                     self.indices[probe] = Pos::new(index, hash);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1735)                     continue 'outer;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1736)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1737) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1738)                 dist += 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1739)             });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1740) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1741)             do_insert_phase_two(&mut self.indices, probe, Pos::new(index, hash));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1742)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1743)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1744) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1745)     fn reinsert_entry_in_order(&mut self, pos: Pos) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1746)         if let Some((_, entry_hash)) = pos.resolve() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1747)             // Find first empty bucket and insert there
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1748)             let mut probe = desired_pos(self.mask, entry_hash);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1749) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1750)             probe_loop!(probe < self.indices.len(), {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1751)                 if self.indices[probe].resolve().is_none() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1752)                     // empty bucket, insert here
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1753)                     self.indices[probe] = pos;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1754)                     return;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1755)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1756)             });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1757)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1758)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1759) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1760)     fn try_reserve_one(&mut self) -> Result<(), MaxSizeReached> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1761)         let len = self.entries.len();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1762) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1763)         if self.danger.is_yellow() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1764)             // Overflow is not a concern here: entries.len() is bounded by
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1765)             // MAX_SIZE (2^15) and LOAD_FACTOR_THRESHOLD is 5, so the product
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1766)             // fits comfortably within a usize.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1767)             if self.entries.len() * LOAD_FACTOR_THRESHOLD >= self.indices.len() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1768)                 // Transition back to green danger level
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1769)                 self.danger.set_green();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1770) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1771)                 // Double the capacity
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1772)                 let new_cap = self.indices.len() * 2;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1773) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1774)                 // Grow the capacity
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1775)                 self.try_grow(new_cap)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1776)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1777)                 self.danger.set_red();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1778) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1779)                 // Rebuild hash table
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1780)                 for index in self.indices.iter_mut() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1781)                     *index = Pos::none();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1782)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1783) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1784)                 self.rebuild();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1785)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1786)         } else if len == self.capacity() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1787)             if len == 0 {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1788)                 let new_raw_cap = 8;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1789)                 self.mask = 8 - 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1790)                 self.indices = vec![Pos::none(); new_raw_cap].into_boxed_slice();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1791)                 self.entries = Vec::with_capacity(usable_capacity(new_raw_cap));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1792)             } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1793)                 let raw_cap = self.indices.len();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1794)                 self.try_grow(raw_cap << 1)?;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1795)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1796)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1797) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1798)         Ok(())
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1799)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1800) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1801)     #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1802)     fn try_grow(&mut self, new_raw_cap: usize) -> Result<(), MaxSizeReached> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1803)         if new_raw_cap > MAX_SIZE {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1804)             return Err(MaxSizeReached::new());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1805)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1806) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1807)         // find first ideally placed element -- start of cluster
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1808)         let mut first_ideal = 0;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1809) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1810)         for (i, pos) in self.indices.iter().enumerate() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1811)             if let Some((_, entry_hash)) = pos.resolve() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1812)                 if 0 == probe_distance(self.mask, entry_hash, i) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1813)                     first_ideal = i;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1814)                     break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1815)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1816)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1817)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1818) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1819)         // visit the entries in an order where we can simply reinsert them
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1820)         // into self.indices without any bucket stealing.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1821)         let old_indices = mem::replace(
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1822)             &mut self.indices,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1823)             vec![Pos::none(); new_raw_cap].into_boxed_slice(),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1824)         );
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1825)         self.mask = new_raw_cap.wrapping_sub(1) as Size;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1826) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1827)         for &pos in &old_indices[first_ideal..] {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1828)             self.reinsert_entry_in_order(pos);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1829)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1830) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1831)         for &pos in &old_indices[..first_ideal] {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1832)             self.reinsert_entry_in_order(pos);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1833)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1834) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1835)         // Reserve additional entry slots
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1836)         let more = self.capacity() - self.entries.len();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1837)         self.entries.reserve_exact(more);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1838)         Ok(())
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1839)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1840) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1841)     #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1842)     fn raw_links(&mut self) -> RawLinks<T> {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1847) /// Removes the `ExtraValue` at the given index.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1848) #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1849) fn remove_extra_value<T>(
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1850)     mut raw_links: RawLinks<T>,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1851)     extra_values: &mut Vec<ExtraValue<T>>,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1852)     idx: usize,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1853) ) -> ExtraValue<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1854)     let prev;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1855)     let next;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1856) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1857)     {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1858)         debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1859)         let extra = &extra_values[idx];
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1860)         prev = extra.prev;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1861)         next = extra.next;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1862)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1863) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1864)     // First unlink the extra value
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1865)     match (prev, next) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1866)         (Link::Entry(prev), Link::Entry(next)) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1867)             debug_assert_eq!(prev, next);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1868) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1869)             raw_links[prev] = None;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1870)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1871)         (Link::Entry(prev), Link::Extra(next)) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1872)             debug_assert!(raw_links[prev].is_some());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1873) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1874)             raw_links[prev].as_mut().unwrap().next = next;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1875) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1876)             debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > next);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1877)             extra_values[next].prev = Link::Entry(prev);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1878)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1879)         (Link::Extra(prev), Link::Entry(next)) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1880)             debug_assert!(raw_links[next].is_some());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1881) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1882)             raw_links[next].as_mut().unwrap().tail = prev;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1883) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1884)             debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > prev);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1885)             extra_values[prev].next = Link::Entry(next);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1886)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1887)         (Link::Extra(prev), Link::Extra(next)) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1888)             debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > next);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1889)             debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > prev);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1890) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1891)             extra_values[prev].next = Link::Extra(next);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1892)             extra_values[next].prev = Link::Extra(prev);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1893)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1894)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1895) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1896)     // Remove the extra value
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1897)     let mut extra = extra_values.swap_remove(idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1898) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1899)     // This is the index of the value that was moved (possibly `extra`)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1900)     let old_idx = extra_values.len();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1901) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1902)     // Update the links
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1903)     if extra.prev == Link::Extra(old_idx) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1904)         extra.prev = Link::Extra(idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1905)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1906) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1907)     if extra.next == Link::Extra(old_idx) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1908)         extra.next = Link::Extra(idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1909)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1910) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1911)     // Check if another entry was displaced. If it was, then the links
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1912)     // need to be fixed.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1913)     if idx != old_idx {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1914)         let next;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1915)         let prev;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1916) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1917)         {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1918)             debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1919)             let moved = &extra_values[idx];
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1920)             next = moved.next;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1921)             prev = moved.prev;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1922)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1923) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1924)         // An entry was moved, we have to the links
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1925)         match prev {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1926)             Link::Entry(entry_idx) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1927)                 // It is critical that we do not attempt to read the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1928)                 // header name or value as that memory may have been
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1929)                 // "released" already.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1930)                 debug_assert!(raw_links[entry_idx].is_some());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1931) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1932)                 let links = raw_links[entry_idx].as_mut().unwrap();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1933)                 links.next = idx;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1934)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1935)             Link::Extra(extra_idx) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1936)                 debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > extra_idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1937)                 extra_values[extra_idx].next = Link::Extra(idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1938)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1939)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1940) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1941)         match next {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1942)             Link::Entry(entry_idx) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1943)                 debug_assert!(raw_links[entry_idx].is_some());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1944) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1945)                 let links = raw_links[entry_idx].as_mut().unwrap();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1946)                 links.tail = idx;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1947)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1948)             Link::Extra(extra_idx) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1949)                 debug_assert!(extra_values.len() > extra_idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1950)                 extra_values[extra_idx].prev = Link::Extra(idx);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1951)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1952)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1953)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1954) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1955)     debug_assert!({
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1956)         for v in &*extra_values {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1957)             assert!(v.next != Link::Extra(old_idx));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1958)             assert!(v.prev != Link::Extra(old_idx));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1959)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1960) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1961)         true
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1962)     });
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1963) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1964)     extra

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1967) fn drain_all_extra_values<T>(
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1968)     raw_links: RawLinks<T>,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1969)     extra_values: &mut Vec<ExtraValue<T>>,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1970)     mut head: usize,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1971) ) -> Vec<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1972)     let mut vec = Vec::new();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1973)     loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1974)         let extra = remove_extra_value(raw_links, extra_values, head);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1975)         vec.push(extra.value);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1976) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1977)         if let Link::Extra(idx) = extra.next {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1978)             head = idx;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1979)         } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1980)             break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1981)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1982)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1983)     vec
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 1984) }

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2004) impl<T> IntoIterator for HeaderMap<T> {

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2007) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2008)     /// Creates a consuming iterator, that is, one that moves keys and values
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2009)     /// out of the map in arbitrary order. The map cannot be used after calling

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2011)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2012)     /// For each yielded item that has `None` provided for the `HeaderName`,

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2014)     /// yielded item. The first yielded item will have `HeaderName` set.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2015)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2016)     /// # Examples
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2017)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2019)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2020)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2021)     /// # use http::header;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2022)     /// # use http::header::*;

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2026)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2027)     /// let mut iter = map.into_iter();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2028)     /// assert_eq!(iter.next(), Some((Some(header::CONTENT_LENGTH), "123".parse().unwrap())));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2029)     /// assert_eq!(iter.next(), Some((Some(header::CONTENT_TYPE), "json".parse().unwrap())));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2030)     /// assert!(iter.next().is_none());

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2061)             extra_values: self.extra_values,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2062)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2063)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2064) }

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2113) impl<T> Extend<(Option<HeaderName>, T)> for HeaderMap<T> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2114)     /// Extend a `HeaderMap` with the contents of another `HeaderMap`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2115)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2116)     /// This function expects the yielded items to follow the same structure as
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2117)     /// `IntoIter`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2118)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2119)     /// # Panics
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2120)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2121)     /// This panics if the first yielded item does not have a `HeaderName`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2122)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2123)     /// # Examples
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2124)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2125)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2126)     /// # use http::header::*;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2127)     /// let mut map = HeaderMap::new();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2128)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2129)     /// map.insert(ACCEPT, "text/plain".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2130)     /// map.insert(HOST, "hello.world".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2131)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2132)     /// let mut extra = HeaderMap::new();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2133)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2134)     /// extra.insert(HOST, "foo.bar".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2135)     /// extra.insert(COOKIE, "hello".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2136)     /// extra.append(COOKIE, "world".parse().unwrap());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2137)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2138)     /// map.extend(extra);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2139)     ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2140)     /// assert_eq!(map["host"], "foo.bar");
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2141)     /// assert_eq!(map["accept"], "text/plain");
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2142)     /// assert_eq!(map["cookie"], "hello");
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2143)     ///

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2148)     /// assert_eq!(2, v.iter().count());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2149)     /// ```
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2150)     fn extend<I: IntoIterator<Item = (Option<HeaderName>, T)>>(&mut self, iter: I) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2151)         let mut iter = iter.into_iter();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2152) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2153)         // Reserve capacity similar to the (HeaderName, T) impl.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2154)         // Keys may be already present or show multiple times in the iterator.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2155)         // Reserve the entire hint lower bound if the map is empty.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2156)         // Otherwise reserve half the hint (rounded up), so the map
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2157)         // will only resize twice in the worst case.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2158)         let hint = if self.is_empty() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2159)             iter.size_hint().0
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2160)         } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2161)             (iter.size_hint().0 + 1) / 2
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2162)         };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2163) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2164)         // Clamp the hint so an over-estimate cannot overflow `reserve`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2165)         let max_reserve = usable_capacity(MAX_SIZE).saturating_sub(self.entries.len());
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2166)         let reserve = hint.min(max_reserve);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2167) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2168)         self.reserve(reserve);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2169) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2170)         // The structure of this is a bit weird, but it is mostly to make the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2171)         // borrow checker happy.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2172)         let (mut key, mut val) = match iter.next() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2173)             Some((Some(key), val)) => (key, val),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2174)             Some((None, _)) => panic!("expected a header name, but got None"),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2175)             None => return,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2176)         };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2177) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2178)         'outer: loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2179)             let mut entry = match self.try_entry2(key).expect("size overflows MAX_SIZE") {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2180)                 Entry::Occupied(mut e) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2181)                     // Replace all previous values while maintaining a handle to
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2182)                     // the entry.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2183)                     e.insert(val);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2184)                     e
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2185)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2186)                 Entry::Vacant(e) => e.insert_entry(val),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2187)             };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2188) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2189)             // As long as `HeaderName` is none, keep inserting the value into
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2190)             // the current entry
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2191)             loop {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2192)                 match iter.next() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2193)                     Some((Some(k), v)) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2194)                         key = k;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2195)                         val = v;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2196)                         continue 'outer;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2197)                     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2198)                     Some((None, v)) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2199)                         entry.append(v);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2200)                     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2201)                     None => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2202)                         return;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2203)                     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2204)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2205)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2206)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2207)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2208) }
