^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2272) /// phase 2 is post-insert where we forward-shift `Pos` in the indices.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2273) ///
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2274) /// returns the number of displaced elements
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2275) #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2276) fn do_insert_phase_two(indices: &mut [Pos], mut probe: usize, mut old_pos: Pos) -> usize {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2277)     let mut num_displaced = 0;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2278) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2279)     probe_loop!(probe < indices.len(), {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2280)         let pos = &mut indices[probe];
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2281) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2282)         if pos.is_none() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2283)             *pos = old_pos;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2284)             break;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2285)         } else {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2286)             num_displaced += 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2287)             old_pos = mem::replace(pos, old_pos);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2288)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2289)     });

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2291)     num_displaced
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2292) }

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2294) #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2295) fn append_value<T>(

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2388)     fn next_unsafe(&mut self) -> Option<(*const HeaderName, *mut T)> {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2389)         use self::Cursor::*;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2390) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2391)         if self.cursor.is_none() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2392)             if (self.entry + 1) >= self.entries_len {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2393)                 return None;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2394)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2395) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2396)             self.entry += 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2397)             self.cursor = Some(Cursor::Head);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2398)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2399) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2400)         // SAFETY: `self.entry < self.entries_len`, and the iterator has
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2401)         // exclusive access to the underlying map for `'a`, so the `entries`
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2402)         // allocation remains valid for the lifetime of the iterator.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2403)         let entry = unsafe { self.entries.add(self.entry) };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2404) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2405)         match self.cursor.unwrap() {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2406)             Head => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2407)                 // SAFETY: `entry` points at a live bucket in `entries`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2408)                 self.cursor = unsafe { (*entry).links }.map(|l| Values(l.next));
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2409)                 // SAFETY: `entry` points at a live bucket, and the iterator only
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2410)                 // yields each slot at most once, so materializing these field
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2411)                 // pointers does not alias another yielded `&mut T`.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2412)                 Some(unsafe {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2413)                     (
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2414)                         ptr::addr_of!((*entry).key),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2415)                         ptr::addr_of_mut!((*entry).value),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2416)                     )
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2417)                 })
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2418)             }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2419)             Values(idx) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2420)                 // SAFETY: `idx` comes from the `links` chain stored in a live
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2421)                 // bucket / extra value, so it points at a live `extra_values`
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2422)                 // slot for the duration of iteration.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2423)                 let extra = unsafe { self.extra_values.add(idx) };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2424) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2425)                 // SAFETY: `extra` points at a live extra value.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2426)                 match unsafe { (*extra).next } {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2427)                     Link::Entry(_) => self.cursor = None,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2428)                     Link::Extra(i) => self.cursor = Some(Values(i)),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2429)                 }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2430) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2431)                 // SAFETY: `entry` and `extra` both point at live elements in the
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2432)                 // map backing storage, and the iterator only yields each value
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2433)                 // slot at most once.
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2434)                 Some(unsafe {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2435)                     (
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2436)                         ptr::addr_of!((*entry).key),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2437)                         ptr::addr_of_mut!((*extra).value),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2438)                     )

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2442)     }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 2443) }

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3577)             // Extras only
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3578)             (&None, Some(extras)) => extras.size_hint(),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3579)             // No more
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3580)             (&None, &None) => (0, Some(0)),
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3581)         }

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3727) /// The number of steps that `current` is forward of the desired position for hash
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3728) #[inline]

^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3733) #[inline]
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3734) fn hash_elem_using<K>(danger: &Danger, k: &K) -> HashValue
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3735) where
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3736)     K: Hash + ?Sized,
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3737) {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3738)     const MASK: u64 = (MAX_SIZE as u64) - 1;
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3739) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3740)     let hash = match *danger {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3741)         // Safe hash
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3742)         Danger::Red(ref hasher) => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3743)             let mut h = hasher.build_hasher();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3744)             k.hash(&mut h);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3745)             h.finish()
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3746)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3747)         // Fast hash
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3748)         _ => {
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3749)             let mut h = FnvHasher::new();
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3750)             k.hash(&mut h);
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3751)             h.finish()
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3752)         }
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3753)     };
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3754) 
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3755)     HashValue((hash & MASK) as u16)
^ef48a7d (evals 2026-10-22 10:54:37 +0200 3756) }
